## Original PR description

## Background

Nightly main→dev sync (2026-10-16): the oldest exact 100-commit prefix of `dev..main`, tip `302ed2304135a75c295a86e84be9b526c81f803c`, merged into dev base `dee456ab738d400d113590592079e54ae68654fd`. Commit 101 (`efb6ed57`) is excluded. Python: +28124 / -2883 lines, 294 files.

## What changed

- 102 commits over `dee456ab`: 100 unchanged main commits, 1 merge `6ca045f0` (parents `dee456ab`, `302ed230`), 1 signed repair commit `898ccb7d`.
- Repair commit:
  - `gigatoken` added to training/mlm extras; `uv.lock` regenerated (uv 0.7.2; adds gigatoken 0.10.0, awkward 2.14.0, awkward-cpp 57). Dockerfile.ci.dev unchanged.
  - Main-API tests adapted to dev: MTP, get_batch, dynamic prefix caching, mimo, DSA, THD CUDA graph, `max_seqlen_per_dp_cp_rank`.
  - Dev H100 golden values refreshed for `moe/gpt3_moe_mcore_te_ep8_resume_torch_dist_dist_muon` (main #6192).
  - Main's Megatron-FSDP v2 `dbuffer.py`/`parameter_group.py` taken (requester-approved); symmetric-memory tests skip on dev's PyTorch.
  - Review 5570704991: `HybridStack` raises `ValueError` for CP>1 + `linear_cp_layout="contiguous"` with GDN/KDA layers (they follow `cp_partition_mode`, zigzag by default; main #6776). New CP=2 test, 4 cases.
  - Review 5573599695: the MTP hybrid stub config sets `cross_entropy_loss_fusion=False` and `cross_entropy_fusion_impl="native"`, read by dev #8105's `HybridModel.forward`.

## Details

- Conflicts: 184 files (120 golden JSON), resolved by hand. CODEOWNERS, copy-pr-bot.yaml, Dockerfile.ci.dev equal dev; `megatron/training/training.py` from main (sync-guide override). No dev-deleted files restored.

## Strict review — changes required; review incomplete

**Findings: CRITICAL: 7, IMPORTANT: 1, SUGGESTION: 0.** Overall risk is **high**. The most impactful finding is an SSRF bypass in remote image fetching. The inspected multimodal paths also contain deterministic construction failures and an image-feature indexing error.

**The remaining inspection task cannot be completed within this bounded run and its final continuation.** The changeset contains 589 files and a 2.7-MB diff; substantial source, tests, and configuration changes remain uninspected. The required full-file, identifier-use, and cross-component checks are consequently unfinished. The task remains pending rather than being falsely marked complete. This is not clearance of the remaining changes, and no coverage-policy override is claimed. All currently validated findings are included below; `review_progress` records the outstanding work.

No tests were executed. Acceptance checks below are proposed; suggested code requires author verification.

### Suggested structural corrections

#### Findings 2–3: construct the optional hybrid MoE spec conditionally

In [get_hybrid_layer_spec_te](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/examples/multimodal/layer_specs.py#L131-L220), replace the configuration extraction/assertion with:

```python
moe_layer = IdentityOp
if config is not None and config.num_moe_experts is not None:
    moe_layer = ModuleSpec(
        module=MoETransformerLayer,
        submodules=TransformerLayerSubmodules(
            pre_mlp_layernorm=TENorm,
            mlp=get_moe_module_spec(
                use_te=True,
                num_experts=config.num_moe_experts,
                moe_grouped_gemm=config.moe_grouped_gemm,
            ),
            mlp_bda=get_bias_dropout_add,
        ),
    )
```

Replace the current eager `moe_layer=ModuleSpec(...)` argument with `moe_layer=moe_layer`. Leave the Mamba, attention, and dense-MLP specs unchanged. Dense patterns never select the MoE slot; expert patterns receive the configured builder. This also removes the unsupported helper keyword.

#### Finding 4: validate individual layers within a block spec

In [LLaVAModel.__init__](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/multimodal/llava_model.py#L183-L200), import `TransformerBlockSubmodules` from `megatron.core.transformer.transformer_block`. Replace the assertions inside the non-hybrid branch with:

```python
layer_specs = (
    language_transformer_layer_spec.layer_specs
    if isinstance(language_transformer_layer_spec, TransformerBlockSubmodules)
    else [language_transformer_layer_spec]
)
for layer_spec in layer_specs:
    assert isinstance(layer_spec.submodules, TransformerLayerSubmodules)
    attention = layer_spec.submodules.self_attention
    assert isinstance(attention.submodules, SelfAttentionSubmodules)
    assert (
        attention.submodules.core_attention == TEDotProductAttention and HAVE_TE
    ), "Sequence/Context Parallelism is supported only with TE DotProductAttention."
```

Pass the original block spec unchanged to GPTModel. This retains the per-layer check and validates every local layer rather than bypassing the TE restriction for blocks.

#### Finding 5: preserve pipeline coordinates when creating the block spec

Add these arguments to the new [get_gpt_decoder_block_spec call](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/examples/multimodal/model.py#L139-L145):

```python
vp_stage=vp_stage,
pp_rank=pg_collection.pp.rank() if pg_collection is not None else None,
```

The absent-collection case retains the helper's existing fallback; supplied groups determine the PP rank.

#### Finding 7: count the features the encoder actually emits

In the dynamic-resolution loop of [expand_image_tokens](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/inference/model_inference_wrappers/multimodal/vlm_inference_wrapper.py#L171-L187), replace the count calculation with the contract already used by LLaVA's dynamic preprocessing:

```python
num_embeddings = (h // patch_dim) * (w // patch_dim)
if not module._drop_vision_class_token:
    num_embeddings += module._class_token_len
if do_pixel_shuffle:
    num_embeddings //= 4
if module._conv_merging:
    num_embeddings //= 4
per_image_embeddings.append(num_embeddings)
```

This uses the resolved class-token count, including constructor/configuration overrides. Native merger configuration must also be wired consistently as described in finding 6. Do not unconditionally add CLS tokens or divide every encoder's count by four. Retained-CLS plus pixel shuffle is not a newly supported combination: the current pixel-shuffle implementation expects patch-only inputs.

#### Finding 8: recognize only the deleted built-in metadata identities

Before the custom-class import in [_get_tokenizer_model_class](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/tokenizers/megatron_tokenizer.py#L182-L185), add narrow compatibility handling:

```python
legacy_text_models = {
    "default_tokenizer": "DefaultTokenizerText",
    "gpt_tokenizer": "GPTTokenizer",
    "bert_tokenizer": "BertTokenizer",
    "mamba_tokenizer": "MambaTokenizer",
    "t5_tokenizer": "T5Tokenizer",
}
legacy_text_identities = {
    (f"megatron.core.tokenizers.text.models.{module}", name)
    for module, name in legacy_text_models.items()
}
identity = (metadata.get("class_path"), metadata.get("class_name"))
if identity in legacy_text_identities:
    from megatron.core.tokenizers.text import MegatronTokenizerText

    return MegatronTokenizerText
if identity == (
    "megatron.core.tokenizers.vision.models.default_tokenizer",
    "DefaultTokenizerVision",
):
    from megatron.core.tokenizers.vision import MegatronTokenizerVision

    return MegatronTokenizerVision
```

Keep the current import behavior for other explicit class identities and the generic fallback for metadata without a class. The inspected deleted built-ins were thin wrappers around these generic classes.

- FDecaYed · CHANGES_REQUESTED · 2026-10-19T11:13:49Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#pullrequestreview-5570704991)

One merge-specific issue to address before merging: with CP>1, hybrid KDA, `linear_cp_layout=contiguous`, and the default `cp_partition_mode=zigzag`, the [new batch path](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/pretrain_hybrid.py#L228) supplies contiguous shards, but the [retained chunkwise path](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/ssm/gated_delta_net/kda.py#L235) converts them as zigzag again, scrambling token order.

For this sync, I suggest rejecting contiguous hybrid CP for the affected GDN/KDA layers until the layout contracts are integrated. Existing dev paths and the new Mamba support can stay unchanged.

Confirmed with a CPU-only source-prefix probe; no full GPU numerical test.

**Sync author note on review 5556506430 (head `d743194c`)**

I traced all 8 findings to source. Each flagged path is semantically identical to `main` at the slice tip `302ed230`. Every one came in verbatim with a slice commit, and none was introduced by the merge resolution (`6ca045f0`) or the repair commit (`d743194c`):

| # | Finding | Origin on main | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | SSRF re-resolution in `_extract_image_url_bytes` | 881f489f (#6260) | function body byte-identical to main |
| 2 | dense hybrid config hits the `num_moe_experts` assert | 881f489f (#6260) | `examples/multimodal/layer_specs.py` == main |
| 3 | unsupported `moe_use_legacy_grouped_gemm` kwarg | 881f489f (#6260) | main's `get_moe_module_spec` lacks it too |
| 4 | LLaVA SP/CP validator vs `TransformerBlockSubmodules` | 881f489f (#6260) | validator region == main |
| 5 | `get_gpt_decoder_block_spec` call missing `vp_stage`/`pp_rank` | 881f489f (#6260) | call site == main (formatting-only diff) |
| 6 | native encoders unreachable via `get_vision_model_config` | 881f489f (#6260) | `config.py`, `encoder_registry.py` == main |
| 7 | retained CLS tokens missing in `expand_image_tokens` | 881f489f (#6260) | `vlm_inference_wrapper.py`, `radio.py` == main |
| 8 | metadata naming deleted built-in tokenizer classes | e4331713 (#6622) | `megatron_tokenizer.py` == main |

The same defects therefore exist on `main` today. I confirmed findings 2, 3, 4, 5 and 8 from source; I did not reproduce 1, 6 or 7. The sync keeps main's content unchanged, so I'm not patching these on `dev`: a dev-only patch would make dev differ from main and conflict with the next sync. The fixes should land on `main` and reach `dev` through a later sync. The review itself says it is incomplete (589 files).

- svcnemo-autobot · comment · 2026-10-19T12:43:38Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#issuecomment-6267491666)

@FDecaYed thanks, you're right. When CP>1 and `linear_cp_layout="contiguous"` (added by main #6776), dev's GDN/KDA layers still take their CP input layout from `cp_partition_mode`, which defaults to zigzag. They re-partition shards that are already contiguous, which scrambles token order.

The fix is in the repair commit, now `c5f94462`:
- `HybridStack` raises a `ValueError` when all three hold: CP>1, `linear_cp_layout="contiguous"`, and the layer pattern contains GDN or KDA. The error tells the user to set `linear_cp_layout="zigzag"`. Mamba with contiguous and GDN with zigzag still build.
- A new test, `tests/unit_tests/ssm/test_hybrid_block.py::TestHybridBlock::test_contiguous_linear_cp_layout_with_gdn_kda`, covers this at CP=2 with 4 cases.

`tests/unit_tests/ssm` on 8×H100 at `c5f94462`: 321 passed, 8 skipped, 0 failed. CI on the new head is pending. Could you take another look?

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623540)

File: `megatron/core/inference/text_generation_server/dynamic_text_gen_server/endpoints/chat_completions.py` · line 296

**[CRITICAL Correctness] Pin the validated destination through the actual HTTP connection**

**Failure:** The public-address check resolves the hostname with `gethostbyname`, but `urllib` subsequently resolves the original URL again. In a proxy-free server, an attacker-controlled hostname can return a public A record for validation and `127.0.0.1` for connection establishment. Posting an `image_url` such as `http://attacker.example:8080/private` therefore makes the frontend issue a GET to a loopback service. The [chat endpoint invokes this fetch before submitting inference](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/inference/text_generation_server/dynamic_text_gen_server/endpoints/chat_completions.py#L606-L636); later image-decoding failure does not undo that request. Mixed A/AAAA answers also escape a check examining only one IPv4 result. Expected: the address actually contacted must satisfy the public-address policy.

**Correction:** Change `_extract_image_url_bytes` and its HTTP transport so resolution, validation, and connection use the same approved address. Validate every candidate the transport may select; prevent unchecked re-resolution and proxy routing. Preserve the original hostname for HTTP Host and HTTPS SNI/certificate verification. The exact transport implementation needs verification; rewriting an HTTPS URL to an IP alone is not a complete fix.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** With a mocked resolver returning public first and loopback on a subsequent lookup, assert that no connection or HTTP request reaches loopback. Repeat with a public A/private AAAA combination. Retain rejection of direct private/link-local destinations and 301/302/303/307/308 redirects, the five-second timeout, and the 20-MiB response cap. Public HTTP/HTTPS image URLs and base64 data URLs should still work; HTTPS must still verify the requested hostname. These are static attack-path reasoning and proposed tests, not executed results.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623544)

File: `examples/multimodal/layer_specs.py` · line 149

**[CRITICAL Correctness] Keep dense hybrid configs out of the MoE-only branch**

**Failure:** The [provider now passes `language_config` for every Nemotron5 hybrid](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/examples/multimodal/model.py#L135-L138). A normal dense `nemotron5-hybrid-8b` configuration has `num_moe_experts=None`, so this assertion aborts model construction. Previously the helper constructed the Mamba/attention/dense-MLP stack without requiring experts. Passing `config=None` is not a workaround: the new MoE expression is evaluated eagerly too.

**Correction:** In `get_hybrid_layer_spec_te`, construct the MoE spec only when a non-null expert count is supplied; otherwise leave its slot at `IdentityOp`, the dataclass default. Do not merely delete the assertion and call the MoE helper with `None`: its [backend contract asserts a non-null expert count](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/gpt/moe_module_specs.py#L64-L71). The review body supplies a conditional replacement that also uses the current helper signature.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** Build the spec with `config=None` and with a dense config, for both `padding=False` and `True`; both must succeed without invoking the MoE helper. Retain Mamba/attention/dense-MLP specs and causal versus padding-causal masks. Construct a small dense `M*-` hybrid and verify its layer types and state-dict keys remain unchanged. With eight experts, the MoE slot must instead contain the configured builder and an `E` layer must construct rather than silently become an identity.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623547)

File: `examples/multimodal/layer_specs.py` · line 216

**[CRITICAL Correctness] Call the dev MoE helper with its supported signature**

**Failure:** With a valid MoE config, for example `num_moe_experts=8`, spec construction reaches this call and raises `TypeError: get_moe_module_spec() got an unexpected keyword argument 'moe_use_legacy_grouped_gemm'`. The [inspected helper accepts only `use_te`, `num_experts`, and `moe_grouped_gemm`](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/gpt/moe_module_specs.py#L40-L61). Consequently the new Nemotron6-MoE provider path cannot construct its spec.

**Correction:** Remove this unsupported keyword from this call, not from unrelated APIs. Keep the returned `MlpBuilder` in `TransformerLayerSubmodules.mlp`; that is the current contract. The conditional patch in the review body combines this change with the separate dense-config repair.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** With TE available, call the helper using an eight-expert configuration with grouped GEMM both enabled and disabled. Each call must succeed and select the corresponding experts implementation, then construct a small hybrid containing an `E` layer with eight configured experts. Dense/`None` configurations must continue to avoid this MoE-only call, and attention padding-mask selection must remain unchanged. These checks are proposed, not run.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623550)

File: `examples/multimodal/model.py` · line 140

**[CRITICAL Correctness] Adapt LLaVA's SP/CP validation to the new block-spec return type**

**Failure:** Selecting `num_experts` now returns a `TransformerBlockSubmodules` object. With sequence parallelism or CP greater than one, [LLaVAModel immediately reads `.submodules` from that object](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/multimodal/llava_model.py#L183-L200), raising `AttributeError`. The [block dataclass has `layer_specs` and `layer_norm`, not `submodules`](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/transformer/transformer_block.py#L218-L235). GPTModel itself supports this block representation, so rejecting it before construction defeats the new branch.

**Correction:** Update the non-hybrid SP/CP validator in `LLaVAModel.__init__` to inspect each local layer spec for a block input and the single layer for the existing ModuleSpec input. Keep the TE-attention restriction; do not simply skip validation for blocks. Pass the original spec unchanged to GPTModel. The review body contains the normalization/checking patch.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** Under initialized groups and TE, construct a small non-hybrid MoE LLaVA using the returned block spec at TP=2/SP=true and separately CP=2. Construction must not raise this AttributeError. A block whose second layer uses non-TE core attention must still fail the supported-backend check. Retain the per-layer ModuleSpec cases in [test_process_embedding_token_parallel](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/tests/unit_tests/models/test_llava_model.py#L817-L888), including its padded THD and unpadded SBHD output-shape assertions.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623552)

File: `examples/multimodal/model.py` · line 145

**[CRITICAL Correctness] Forward the virtual pipeline stage when slicing MoE specs**

**Failure:** For a non-hybrid MoE model with PP=2 and VPP=2, `model_provider(vp_stage=0, ...)` calls the new block builder without that stage. The builder slices local layers using `get_transformer_layer_offset`, which [asserts that `vp_stage` is provided under VPP](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/transformer/transformer_layer.py#L201-L208). This fails before LLaVA construction, even with SP/CP disabled. The [training caller supplies the stage explicitly](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/training/training.py#L3664-L3678).

**Correction:** Add `vp_stage=vp_stage` and `pp_rank=pg_collection.pp.rank() if pg_collection is not None else None` to this call, as shown in the review body. The block must be sliced using the same pipeline coordinates as the language model, while absent explicit groups retain the existing fallback.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** Use a four-layer config with PP=2/VPP=2 and a mixed dense/MoE pattern. Build each physical/virtual stage and assert that each gets its intended one-layer spec, collectively covering all four layers exactly once without the assertion. Use a supplied PP collection to ensure selection follows that collection rather than a different global rank. Retain PP=1 and PP=2/no-VPP layer counts and `vp_stage=None` behavior when VPP is disabled. This is independent of the block-type validation failure above.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623555)

File: `examples/multimodal/model.py` · line 196

**[CRITICAL Correctness] Complete construction support for the newly selected native encoders**

**Failure:** The added Pixtral/Qwen/Kimi branches are unreachable through this provider. With dynamic resolution and `vision_model_type='qwen-vl'`, the earlier call to [get_vision_model_config](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/examples/multimodal/config.py#L188-L333) raises `ValueError: unknown vision model type qwen-vl`. Static resolution fails even earlier in `get_num_image_embeddings`. Even a direct LLaVAModel caller supplying its own native vision config eventually hits the [unconditional geometry-helper call](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/multimodal/llava_model.py#L495-L506), whose implementation rejects all four new names.

**Correction:** Add native-only architecture resolution to `get_vision_model_config`, using the corresponding [encoder registry entries](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/vision/encoder_registry.py#L290-L378), and extend the geometry contract used by both provider and LLaVA constructor. Native encoders have no CLS tokens; plain Pixtral emits one token per patch, whereas Pixtral-Large, Qwen, and Kimi emit one per 2×2 patch group. Wire the merger flag consistently for dynamic accounting. Fixing only the config rejection leaves the next constructor failure intact. A complete integration patch requires verification, so no speculative registry-wide rewrite is suggested.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** Parameterize provider construction over the four new names and assert expected architecture fields plus successful lightweight LLaVA construction. For a 4×4 patch grid, assert 16 tokens for plain Pixtral and four for each merged encoder, matching actual output. Preserve CLIP's retained/dropped CLS behavior, SigLIP's no-CLS behavior, existing RADIO configuration/overrides, and existing pixel-shuffle accounting; do not replace those established defaults wholesale with registry defaults.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:43Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623559)

File: `megatron/core/inference/model_inference_wrappers/multimodal/vlm_inference_wrapper.py` · line 184

**[CRITICAL Implementation] Include retained class tokens in dynamic image expansion**

**Failure:** For dynamic RADIO with patch size 16, eight retained CLS tokens, no pixel shuffle, and two images sized 32×32 and 32×64, this code allocates 4+8 image positions. The encoder actually emits 12+16 features: [RADIO prepends CLS tokens per image](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/vision/radio.py#L400-L423), and LLaVA removes them only when requested. The second image's mask consequently starts at feature four instead of twelve, so it consumes features from the first image. The later maximum-index guard does not detect under-counting. Expected: expansion and per-image offsets must match the post-encoder/post-shuffle feature stream.

**Correction:** In `expand_image_tokens`, add the resolved `module._class_token_len` only when `_drop_vision_class_token` is false, then apply configured pixel-shuffle and native-merger reductions. The review body gives the replacement consistent with [LLaVA's dynamic count contract](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/megatron/core/models/multimodal/llava_model.py#L706-L725). Do not blanket-add CLS tokens for no-CLS encoders or blanket-divide unmerged encoders by four.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** For the two-image example, assert counts `[12,16]`, a second-image offset of 12, and that distinguishable per-image feature sentinels land only in their own positions. With CLS dropped, expect `[4,8]`. For dropped-CLS RADIO with 4×4 and 4×8 patch grids plus pixel shuffle, expect `[4,8]`; no-CLS native 2×2 mergers should also use `[4,8]` when configured, while unmerged Pixtral uses `[16,32]`. Retain the static `num_tiles × img_seq_len` path. Tests are proposed, not executed.

- svcnvidia-nemo-ci · comment · 2026-10-17T05:49:44Z · [source](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/8305#discussion_r4301623562)

File: `megatron/core/tokenizers/megatron_tokenizer.py` · line 184

**[IMPORTANT Compatibility] Resolve metadata referring to the deleted built-in tokenizer classes**

**Failure:** Previously valid metadata such as `{'library':'null-text','model_type':'gpt','class_name':'GPTTokenizer','class_path':'megatron.core.tokenizers.text.models.gpt_tokenizer'}` loaded successfully with `vocab_size=16`. The [previous metadata writer could emit that class identity](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/dee456ab738d400d113590592079e54ae68654fd/megatron/core/tokenizers/megatron_tokenizer.py#L104-L165). This resolver now imports that identity, but the module was deleted, so loading raises ModuleNotFoundError. The same applies to the other deleted built-in text wrappers and DefaultTokenizerVision. Expected: existing tokenizer metadata remains loadable after this simplification.

**Correction:** Before importing explicit classes, map only the six deleted built-in identities to MegatronTokenizerText or MegatronTokenizerVision, or restore equivalent compatibility modules. The review body contains a narrow resolver patch. Do not ignore all class metadata: that would break the newly supported custom-class path.

**Preservation / proposed acceptance:** Load the literal null-text metadata above without tokenizer files and assert `tokenize('1 2') == [1,2]` and `eod == 15`. Parameterize the five old text identities, and exercise the old vision identity with the null-multimodal backend. Metadata without explicit class fields and newly written generic metadata must still load. Retain [test_own_metadata_class](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/blob/d743194ca190221385d61008096bc9f797d3097f/tests/unit_tests/tokenizers/test_tokenizer.py#L479-L498)'s assertion that the result is an instance of CustomTokenizerClass. These are proposed checks, not run results.
